Code, and Cursor. Generate vector embeddings locally with sentence-transformers. Expose everything via an MCP server that any AI tool can query.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cblockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eOpen core:\u003c/strong\u003e The local pipeline (import, embed, summarize, search, MCP server) is free and AGPL-licensed. Cloud sync and hosted MCP are on the roadmap at \u003ca href=\"https://mychatarchive.com\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003emychatarchive.com\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/blockquote\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eWhy MyChatArchive\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-why-mychatarchive\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Why MyChatArchive\" href=\"#why-mychatarchive\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eYour archive, searchable.\u003c/strong\u003e Drop your ChatGPT, Claude, or Grok exports into the import folder, or let MyChatArchive auto-discover Claude Code and Cursor sessions from your machine. Full transcripts, local embeddings, MCP server. No cloud required.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cmarkdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\u003ctable\u003e\n\u003cthead\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003cth\u003e\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003e\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/thead\u003e\n\u003ctbody\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eLossless\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eFull message transcripts, not extracted summaries. You can always search the original.\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eLocal-first\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eSingle SQLite file. Embeddings run on your machine. Core needs no API keys.\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eDeveloper-native\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eAuto-discovers Claude Code sessions and Cursor conversations from your local machine on day one.\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eMCP server\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eClaude Desktop, Cursor, Claude Code, and any MCP client can search your archive.\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/markdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eQuick Start\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-quick-start\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Quick Start\" href=\"#quick-start\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"git clone https://github.com/1ch1n/mychatarchive.git\ncd mychatarchive\npip install .\n\n# 1. Set up (creates drop folder, configures auto-discovery)\nmychatarchive init\n\n# 2. Import everything in one command\n#    Auto-discovers Claude Code + Cursor, scans your drop folder\nmychatarchive sync\n\n# 3. (Optional) Generate thread summaries for richer context retrieval\n#    Needs an API key: set OPENROUTER_API_KEY or ANTHROPIC_API_KEY\nmychatarchive summarize\n\n# 4. Generate local embeddings\nmychatarchive embed\n\n# 5. Start the MCP server\nmychatarchive serve\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003egit clone https://github.com/1ch1n/mychatarchive.git\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003ecd\u003c/span\u003e mychatarchive\npip install \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e.\u003c/span\u003e\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e 1. Set up (creates drop folder, configures auto-discovery)\u003c/span\u003e\nmychatarchive init\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e 2. Import everything in one command\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e    Auto-discovers Claude Code + Cursor, scans your drop folder\u003c/span\u003e\nmychatarchive sync\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e 3. (Optional) Generate thread summaries for richer context retrieval\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e    Needs an API key: set OPENROUTER_API_KEY or ANTHROPIC_API_KEY\u003c/span\u003e\nmychatarchive summarize\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e 4. Generate local embeddings\u003c/span\u003e\nmychatarchive embed\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e 5. Start the MCP server\u003c/span\u003e\nmychatarchive serve\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThen connect from Claude Desktop or Cursor: run \u003ccode\u003emychatarchive mcp-config\u003c/code\u003e and add the output to your client config. That's it.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eWhat You Get\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca does\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/thead\u003e\n\u003ctbody\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003esearch_brain\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eSemantic search by meaning across all conversations\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003esearch_recent\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eRecent conversations and captured thoughts by time range\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eget_context\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eFull context bundle for a topic: related threads, LLM summaries, thoughts\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003ecapture_thought\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eSave a thought or note with auto-embedding for future retrieval\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eget_profile\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eSnapshot of your recent focus areas, thread summaries, and thoughts\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eget_current_datetime\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eCurrent UTC datetime, injected into every tool response\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/markdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eAll search tools support filtering by platform, time range (\u003ccode\u003ehours_back\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003esince\u003c/code\u003e), and thread group. Sort by relevance or recency.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eExample:\u003c/strong\u003e Ask Claude \"What did I decide about the database architecture last month?\" and it searches your actual conversation history semantically.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" API for thread summaries (optional but recommended for \u003ccode\u003eget_profile\u003c/code\u003e)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"mychatarchive sync           # import from all sources\nmychatarchive summarize      # LLM thread summaries (optional, needs API key)\nmychatarchive embed          # generate vector embeddings locally\nmychatarchive serve          # start MCP server\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003emychatarchive sync           \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e import from all sources\u003c/span\u003e\nmychatarchive summarize      \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e LLM thread summaries (optional, needs API key)\u003c/span\u003e\nmychatarchive embed          \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e generate vector embeddings locally\u003c/span\u003e\nmychatarchive serve          \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e start MCP server\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eShortcut:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"mychatarchive sync --embed   # sync + embed in one shot\nmychatarchive serve\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003emychatarchive sync --embed   \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e sync + embed in one shot\u003c/span\u003e\nmychatarchive serve\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe pipeline is incremental. Re-run \u003ccode\u003esync\u003c/code\u003e any time -- SHA1 dedup means it's always safe. New messages get embedded on the next \u003ccode\u003eembed\u003c/code\u003e run without \u003ccode\u003e--force\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eSync\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-sync\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Sync\" href=\"#sync\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"mychatarchive sync           # import from all sources\nmychatarchive sync --embed   # sync + generate embeddings in one shot\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003emychatarchive sync           \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e import from all sources\u003c/span\u003e\nmychatarchive sync --embed   \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e sync + generate embeddings in one shot\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003esync\u003c/code\u003e imports in three layers:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003col dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eAuto-discovery\u003c/strong\u003e -- Claude Code sessions (\u003ccode\u003e~/.claude/projects/\u003c/code\u003e) and Cursor conversations from local databases. Enabled by default, toggleable in \u003ccode\u003einit\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eDrop folder\u003c/strong\u003e -- anything in \u003ccode\u003e~/.mychatarchive/imports/\u003c/code\u003e. Drop your ChatGPT, Claude, or Grok export JSON here; format is auto-detected. Subdirectories scanned recursively.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eNamed sources\u003c/strong\u003e -- custom paths or NAS shares you've configured with \u003ccode\u003emychatarchive sources add\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eAll three deduplicate into the same archive via SHA1 hashing.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cblockquote\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eNote:\u003c/strong\u003e Auto-discovery covers Claude Code (the terminal agent) and Cursor. Claude web, mobile, and desktop app conversations require a manual export from Anthropic's settings -- drop the file in your imports folder and run \u003ccode\u003esync\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/blockquote\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eSummarize\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-summarize\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Summarize\" href=\"#summarize\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eGenerate LLM thread summaries for richer context retrieval and the \u003ccode\u003eget_profile\u003c/code\u003e MCP tool.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"mychatarchive summarize                              # default model via OpenRouter\nmychatarchive summarize --model gpt-4o-mini          # specify model\nmychatarchive summarize --key sk-...                 # pass API key inline\nmychatarchive summarize --limit 50                   # process first 50 threads (for testing)\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003emychatarchive summarize                              \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e default model via OpenRouter\u003c/span\u003e\nmychatarchive summarize --model gpt-4o-mini          \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e specify model\u003c/span\u003e\nmychatarchive summarize --key sk-...                 \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e pass API key inline\u003c/span\u003e\nmychatarchive summarize --limit 50                   \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e process first 50 threads (for testing)\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eSummaries are stored in SQLite, embedded into their own vector index, and surfaced by \u003ccode\u003eget_context\u003c/code\u003e and \u003ccode\u003eget_profile\u003c/code\u003e. Without summaries, \u003ccode\u003eget_profile\u003c/code\u003e falls back to recent message chunks.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eAPI key:\u003c/strong\u003e Set \u003ccode\u003eOPENROUTER_API_KEY\u003c/code\u003e (default) or \u003ccode\u003eANTHROPIC_API_KEY\u003c/code\u003e, or pass \u003ccode\u003e--key\u003c/code\u003e inline.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eThread Groups\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-thread-groups\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Thread Groups\" href=\"#thread-groups\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eOrganize threads into named groups for scoped search and context retrieval. Useful when your archive mixes personal conversations, coding work, and project threads -- you can scope search to exactly what's relevant.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"# Create groups\nmychatarchive groups create jarvis --description \u0026quot;Daily personal chats\u0026quot;\nmychatarchive groups create coding --description \u0026quot;Dev work and technical threads\u0026quot;\n\n# Browse threads to find IDs\nmychatarchive groups show jarvis\n\n# Add threads\nmychatarchive groups add jarvis \u0026lt;thread_id\u0026gt; \u0026lt;thread_id\u0026gt;\n\n# Scope search to a group\nmychatarchive search \u0026quot;what did I class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\u0026gt;\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\u0026lt;\u003c/span\u003ethread_id\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\u0026gt;\u003c/span\u003e\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Scope search to a group\u003c/span\u003e\nmychatarchive search \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003ewhat did I decide\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e --group jarvis\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e In MCP tools: search_brain(query=\"...\", group=\"jarvis\")\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe \u003ccode\u003egroup\u003c/code\u003e filter works on \u003ccode\u003esearch_brain\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eget_context\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eget_profile\u003c/code\u003e, and the \u003ccode\u003esearch\u003c/code\u003e CLI.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eSensitivity Levels\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-sensitivity-levels\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Sensitivity Levels\" href=\"#sensitivity-levels\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eYears of chat history contain things an agent should not surface on any random turn -- sensitive work, personal matters, private notes. Classify threads into three levels and the archive enforces them in the data access layer:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cmarkdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\u003ctable\u003e\n\u003cthead\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eLevel\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eBehavior\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/thead\u003e\n\u003ctbody\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003epublic\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eDefault. Returned by every search and MCP tool.\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eprivate\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eReturned only when a caller explicitly opts in (\u003ccode\u003einclude_private\u003c/code\u003e on MCP tools, \u003ccode\u003e--include-private\u003c/code\u003e on the CLI).\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003esealed\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eNever returned by the MCP server, under any circumstances.\u003c/strong\u003e CLI-only, and only with an explicit \u003ccode\u003e--include-sealed\u003c/code\u003e flag.\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/markdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"# See where you stand\nmychatarchive classify --list\n\n# Classify one thread (applies immediately)\nmychatarchive classify --thread \u0026lt;id\u0026gt; --level private\n\n# Bulk-classify by keyword -- previews first, applies only with --confirm\nmychatarchive classify --query \u0026quot;project-x\u0026quot; --level sealed\nmychatarchive classify --query \u0026quot;project-x\u0026quot; --level sealed --confirm\n\n# Bulk-classify old threads (last message before a date)\nmychatarchive classify --before 2022-01-01 --level private --confirm\n\n# Search honors levels\nmychatarchive search \u0026quot;query\u0026quot;                    # public only\nmychatarchive search \u0026quot;query\u0026quot; --include-private\nmychatarchive search \u0026quot;query\u0026quot; --include-sealed   # prints a warning\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e See where you stand\u003c/span\u003e\nmychatarchive classify --list\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Classify one thread (applies immediately)\u003c/span\u003e\nmychatarchive classify --thread \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\u0026lt;\u003c/span\u003eid\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e\u0026gt;\u003c/span\u003e --level private\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Bulk-classify by keyword -- previews first, applies only with --confirm\u003c/span\u003e\nmychatarchive classify --query \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003eproject-x\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e --level sealed\nmychatarchive classify --query \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003eproject-x\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e --level sealed --confirm\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Bulk-classify old threads (last message before a date)\u003c/span\u003e\nmychatarchive classify --before 2022-01-01 --level private --confirm\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Search honors levels\u003c/span\u003e\nmychatarchive search \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003equery\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e                    \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e public only\u003c/span\u003e\nmychatarchive search \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003equery\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e --include-private\nmychatarchive search \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003equery\u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e --include-sealed   \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e prints a warning\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eDetails worth knowing:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eEnforcement lives in the data access layer, not in tool handlers. A retrieval call that doesn't name a scope fails closed to public -- including third-party integrations that use \u003ccode\u003emychatarchive.db\u003c/code\u003e directly.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eBulk classification is thread-scoped: one matching message classifies the whole conversation, which is how sensitivity works in practice.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003emychatarchive summarize\u003c/code\u003e (the one command that sends content to an external LLM API) summarizes public threads only by default; \u003ccode\u003e--include-private\u003c/code\u003e opts in, and sealed threads never leave the machine.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eRe-running \u003ccode\u003esync\u003c/code\u003e on a classified thread is safe: newly imported messages inherit the thread's level before anything can read them.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eExisting archives migrate automatically on first use -- every row defaults to \u003ccode\u003epublic\u003c/code\u003e, and a verified backup (\u003ccode\u003e*.pre-v3-*.backup.sqlite\u003c/code\u003e) is written next to the database before the schema changes.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eSealed content is hidden, not encrypted: the text still exists in the SQLite file, its FTS index, and its vectors. Protect the file itself with filesystem permissions. Encryption at rest is a possible future addition.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eSearch from the CLI\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-search-from-the-cli\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Search from the CLI\" href=\"#search-from-the-cli\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"mychatarchive search \u0026quot;database architecture decisions\u0026quot;\nmychatarchive search \u0026quot;python error handling\u0026quot; Supports: \u003ccode\u003e--mode keyword\u003c/code\u003e for FTS, \u003ccode\u003e--platform\u003c/code\u003e for source filter, \u003ccode\u003e--hours\u003c/code\u003e / \u003ccode\u003e--since\u003c/code\u003e for time filter, \u003ccode\u003e--sort time\u003c/code\u003e for newest-first, \u003ccode\u003e--group\u003c/code\u003e for group init\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eInteractive setup (drop folder, auto-discovery, backends)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003emychatarchive sync\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eImport from all sources (auto + drop folder + named)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003emychatarchive sync --embed\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eSync + generate embeddings in one shot\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003emychatarchive import \u0026lt;file|dir\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eImport a single file or directory\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003emychatarchive import --from \u0026lt;name\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eImport from a named source\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003emychatarchive sources add \u0026lt;name\u0026gt; \u0026lt;path\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eAdd a named import source\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003emychatarchive sources list\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eShow all sources (auto + drop + named)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003emychatarchive sources remove \u0026lt;name\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eRemove a source\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003emychatarchive sources rename \u0026lt;old\u0026gt; \u0026lt;new\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eRename a source\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003emychatarchive summarize\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eGenerate LLM thread summaries (needs API key)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003emychatarchive groups list\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eList all thread groups\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003emychatarchive groups create \u0026lt;name\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eCreate a thread group\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003emychatarchive groups add \u0026lt;group\u0026gt; \u0026lt;ids...\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eAdd threads to a group\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003emychatarchive groups show \u0026lt;name\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eShow threads in a group\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003emychatarchive groups delete \u0026lt;name\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eDelete a group (threads are not deleted)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003emychatarchive classify --list\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eShow sensitivity counts per level\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003emychatarchive classify --thread \u0026lt;id\u0026gt; --level \u0026lt;l\u0026gt;\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eClassify a thread (public/private/sealed)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003emychatarchive classify --query \u0026lt;text\u0026gt; --level \u0026lt;l\u0026gt; --confirm\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eBulk-classify threads by keyword\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003emychatarchive classify --before <hr>
<p dir="auto">Import your chat exports from ChatGPT, Claude, Grok, Claude Code, and Cursor. Generate vector embeddings locally with sentence-transformers. Expose everything via an MCP server that any AI tool can query.</p>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Open core:</strong> The local pipeline (import, embed, summarize, search, MCP server) is free and AGPL-licensed. Cloud sync and hosted MCP are on the roadmap at <a href="https://mychatarchive.com" rel="nofollow">mychatarchive.com</a>.</p>
</blockquote>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Why MyChatArchive</h2><a id="user-content-why-mychatarchive" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Why MyChatArchive" href="#why-mychatarchive"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Your archive, searchable.</strong> Drop your ChatGPT, Claude, or Grok exports into the import folder, or let MyChatArchive auto-discover Claude Code and Cursor sessions from your machine. Full transcripts, local embeddings, MCP server. No cloud required.</p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th></th>
<th></th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><strong>Lossless</strong></td>
<td>Full message transcripts, not extracted summaries. You can always search the original.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>Local-first</strong></td>
<td>Single SQLite file. Embeddings run on your machine. Core needs no API keys.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>Developer-native</strong></td>
<td>Auto-discovers Claude Code sessions and Cursor conversations from your local machine on day one.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>MCP server</strong></td>
<td>Claude Desktop, Cursor, Claude Code, and any MCP client can search your archive.</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Quick Start</h2><a id="user-content-quick-start" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Quick Start" href="#quick-start"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="git clone https://github.com/1ch1n/mychatarchive.git
cd mychatarchive
pip install .

# 1. Set up (creates drop folder, configures auto-discovery)
mychatarchive init

# 2. Import everything in one command
#    Auto-discovers Claude Code + Cursor, scans your drop folder
mychatarchive sync

# 3. (Optional) Generate thread summaries for richer context retrieval
#    Needs an API key: set OPENROUTER_API_KEY or ANTHROPIC_API_KEY
mychatarchive summarize

# 4. Generate local embeddings
mychatarchive embed

# 5. Start the MCP server
mychatarchive serve"><pre>git clone https://github.com/1ch1n/mychatarchive.git
<span class="pl-c1">cd</span> mychatarchive
pip install <span class="pl-c1">.</span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> 1. Set up (creates drop folder, configures auto-discovery)</span>
mychatarchive init

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> 2. Import everything in one command</span>
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span>    Auto-discovers Claude Code + Cursor, scans your drop folder</span>
mychatarchive sync

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> 3. (Optional) Generate thread summaries for richer context retrieval</span>
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span>    Needs an API key: set OPENROUTER_API_KEY or ANTHROPIC_API_KEY</span>
mychatarchive summarize

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> 4. Generate local embeddings</span>
mychatarchive embed

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> 5. Start the MCP server</span>
mychatarchive serve</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Then connect from Claude Desktop or Cursor: run <code>mychatarchive mcp-config</code> and add the output to your client config. That's it.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">What You Get</h2><a id="user-content-what-you-get" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: What You Get" href="#what-you-get"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Once the MCP server is running, any connected AI tool can call:</p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Tool</th>
<th>What it does</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>search_brain</code></td>
<td>Semantic search by meaning across all conversations</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>search_recent</code></td>
<td>Recent conversations and captured thoughts by time range</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>get_context</code></td>
<td>Full context bundle for a topic: related threads, LLM summaries, thoughts</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>capture_thought</code></td>
<td>Save a thought or note with auto-embedding for future retrieval</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>get_profile</code></td>
<td>Snapshot of your recent focus areas, thread summaries, and thoughts</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>get_current_datetime</code></td>
<td>Current UTC datetime, injected into every tool response</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<p dir="auto">All search tools support filtering by platform, time range (<code>hours_back</code>, <code>since</code>), and thread group. Sort by relevance or recency.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Example:</strong> Ask Claude "What did I decide about the database architecture last month?" and it searches your actual conversation history semantically.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Installation</h2><a id="user-content-installation" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Installation" href="#installation"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">From source (recommended for now)</h3><a id="user-content-from-source-recommended-for-now" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: From source (recommended for now)" href="#from-source-recommended-for-now"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

cd mychatarchive
pip install ."><pre>git clone https://github.com/1ch1n/mychatarchive.git
<span class="pl-c1">cd</span> mychatarchive
pip install <span class="pl-c1">.</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Development install</h3><a id="user-content-development-install" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Development install" href="#development-install"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="pip install -e &quot;.[dev]&quot;"><pre>pip install -e <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>.[dev]<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Requirements</h3><a id="user-content-requirements" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Requirements" href="#requirements"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Python 3.10+</li>
<li>~500MB disk for the embedding model (downloaded once, runs locally)</li>
<li>No API keys needed for: sync, embed, search, serve</li>
<li><code>summarize</code> uses an LLM API for thread summaries (optional but recommended for <code>get_profile</code>)</li>
</ul>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">The Pipeline</h2><a id="user-content-the-pipeline" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: The Pipeline" href="#the-pipeline"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Full workflow:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="mychatarchive sync           # import from all sources
mychatarchive summarize      # LLM thread summaries (optional, needs API key)
mychatarchive embed          # generate vector embeddings locally
mychatarchive serve          # start MCP server"><pre>mychatarchive sync           <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> import from all sources</span>
mychatarchive summarize      <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> LLM thread summaries (optional, needs API key)</span>
mychatarchive embed          <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> generate vector embeddings locally</span>
mychatarchive serve          <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> start MCP server</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Shortcut:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="mychatarchive sync --embed   # sync + embed in one shot
mychatarchive serve"><pre>mychatarchive sync --embed   <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> sync + embed in one shot</span>
mychatarchive serve</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">The pipeline is incremental. Re-run <code>sync</code> any time -- SHA1 dedup means it's always safe. New messages get embedded on the next <code>embed</code> run without <code>--force</code>.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Sync</h2><a id="user-content-sync" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Sync" href="#sync"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="mychatarchive sync           # import from all sources
mychatarchive sync --embed   # sync + generate embeddings in one shot"><pre>mychatarchive sync           <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> import from all sources</span>
mychatarchive sync --embed   <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> sync + generate embeddings in one shot</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><code>sync</code> imports in three layers:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li><strong>Auto-discovery</strong> -- Claude Code sessions (<code>~/.claude/projects/</code>) and Cursor conversations from local databases. Enabled by default, toggleable in <code>init</code>.</li>
<li><strong>Drop folder</strong> -- anything in <code>~/.mychatarchive/imports/</code>. Drop your ChatGPT, Claude, or Grok export JSON here; format is auto-detected. Subdirectories scanned recursively.</li>
<li><strong>Named sources</strong> -- custom paths or NAS shares you've configured with <code>mychatarchive sources add</code>.</li>
</ol>
<p dir="auto">All three deduplicate into the same archive via SHA1 hashing.</p>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Note:</strong> Auto-discovery covers Claude Code (the terminal agent) and Cursor. Claude web, mobile, and desktop app conversations require a manual export from Anthropic's settings -- drop the file in your imports folder and run <code>sync</code>.</p>
</blockquote>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Summarize</h2><a id="user-content-summarize" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Summarize" href="#summarize"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Generate LLM thread summaries for richer context retrieval and the <code>get_profile</code> MCP tool.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="mychatarchive summarize                              # default model via OpenRouter
mychatarchive summarize --model gpt-4o-mini          # specify model
mychatarchive summarize --key sk-...                 # pass API key inline
mychatarchive summarize --limit 50                   # process first 50 threads (for testing)"><pre>mychatarchive summarize                              <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> default model via OpenRouter</span>
mychatarchive summarize --model gpt-4o-mini          <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> specify model</span>
mychatarchive summarize --key sk-...                 <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> pass API key inline</span>
mychatarchive summarize --limit 50                   <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> process first 50 threads (for testing)</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Summaries are stored in SQLite, embedded into their own vector index, and surfaced by <code>get_context</code> and <code>get_profile</code>. Without summaries, <code>get_profile</code> falls back to recent message chunks.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>API key:</strong> Set <code>OPENROUTER_API_KEY</code> (default) or <code>ANTHROPIC_API_KEY</code>, or pass <code>--key</code> inline.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Thread Groups</h2><a id="user-content-thread-groups" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Thread Groups" href="#thread-groups"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Organize threads into named groups for scoped search and context retrieval. Useful when your archive mixes personal conversations, coding work, and project threads -- you can scope search to exactly what's relevant.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Create groups
mychatarchive groups create jarvis --description &quot;Daily personal chats&quot;
mychatarchive groups create coding --description &quot;Dev work and technical threads&quot;

# Browse threads to find IDs
mychatarchive groups show jarvis

# Add threads
mychatarchive groups add jarvis &lt;thread_id&gt; &lt;thread_id&gt;

# Scope search to a group
mychatarchive search &quot;what did I decide&quot; --group jarvis

# In MCP tools: search_brain(query=&quot;...&quot;, group=&quot;jarvis&quot;)"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Create groups</span>
mychatarchive groups create jarvis --description <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>Daily personal chats<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
mychatarchive groups create coding --description <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>Dev work and technical threads<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Browse threads to find IDs</span>
mychatarchive groups show jarvis

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Add threads</span>
mychatarchive groups add jarvis <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>thread_id<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span> <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>thread_id<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Scope search to a group</span>
mychatarchive search <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>what did I decide<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> --group jarvis

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> In MCP tools: search_brain(query="...", group="jarvis")</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">The <code>group</code> filter works on <code>search_brain</code>, <code>get_context</code>, <code>get_profile</code>, and the <code>search</code> CLI.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Sensitivity Levels</h2><a id="user-content-sensitivity-levels" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Sensitivity Levels" href="#sensitivity-levels"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Years of chat history contain things an agent should not surface on any random turn -- sensitive work, personal matters, private notes. Classify threads into three levels and the archive enforces them in the data access layer:</p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Level</th>
<th>Behavior</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>public</code></td>
<td>Default. Returned by every search and MCP tool.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>private</code></td>
<td>Returned only when a caller explicitly opts in (<code>include_private</code> on MCP tools, <code>--include-private</code> on the CLI).</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>sealed</code></td>
<td><strong>Never returned by the MCP server, under any circumstances.</strong> CLI-only, and only with an explicit <code>--include-sealed</code> flag.</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# See where you stand
mychatarchive classify --list

# Classify one thread (applies immediately)
mychatarchive classify --thread &lt;id&gt; --level private

# Bulk-classify by keyword -- previews first, applies only with --confirm
mychatarchive classify --query &quot;project-x&quot; --level sealed
mychatarchive classify --query &quot;project-x&quot; --level sealed --confirm

# Bulk-classify old threads (last message before a date)
mychatarchive classify --before 2022-01-01 --level private --confirm

# Search honors levels
mychatarchive search &quot;query&quot;                    # public only
mychatarchive search &quot;query&quot; --include-private
mychatarchive search &quot;query&quot; --include-sealed   # prints a warning"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> See where you stand</span>
mychatarchive classify --list

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Classify one thread (applies immediately)</span>
mychatarchive classify --thread <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>id<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span> --level private

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Bulk-classify by keyword -- previews first, applies only with --confirm</span>
mychatarchive classify --query <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>project-x<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> --level sealed
mychatarchive classify --query <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>project-x<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> --level sealed --confirm

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Bulk-classify old threads (last message before a date)</span>
mychatarchive classify --before 2022-01-01 --level private --confirm

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Search honors levels</span>
mychatarchive search <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>query<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>                    <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> public only</span>
mychatarchive search <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>query<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> --include-private
mychatarchive search <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>query<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> --include-sealed   <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> prints a warning</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Details worth knowing:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Enforcement lives in the data access layer, not in tool handlers. A retrieval call that doesn't name a scope fails closed to public -- including third-party integrations that use <code>mychatarchive.db</code> directly.</li>
<li>Bulk classification is thread-scoped: one matching message classifies the whole conversation, which is how sensitivity works in practice.</li>
<li><code>mychatarchive summarize</code> (the one command that sends content to an external LLM API) summarizes public threads only by default; <code>--include-private</code> opts in, and sealed threads never leave the machine.</li>
<li>Re-running <code>sync</code> on a classified thread is safe: newly imported messages inherit the thread's level before anything can read them.</li>
<li>Existing archives migrate automatically on first use -- every row defaults to <code>public</code>, and a verified backup (<code>*.pre-v3-*.backup.sqlite</code>) is written next to the database before the schema changes.</li>
<li>Sealed content is hidden, not encrypted: the text still exists in the SQLite file, its FTS index, and its vectors. Protect the file itself with filesystem permissions. Encryption at rest is a possible future addition.</li>
</ul>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Search from the CLI</h2><a id="user-content-search-from-the-cli" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Search from the CLI" href="#search-from-the-cli"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="mychatarchive search &quot;database architecture decisions&quot;
mychatarchive search &quot;python error handling&quot; --mode keyword
mychatarchive search &quot;auth flow&quot; --platform claude_code --group coding
mychatarchive search &quot;what did I build&quot; --hours 168 --sort time
mychatarchive search &quot;api design&quot; --since 2026-01-01"><pre>mychatarchive search <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>database architecture decisions<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
mychatarchive search <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>python error handling<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> --mode keyword
mychatarchive search <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>auth flow<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> --platform claude_code --group coding
mychatarchive search <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>what did I build<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> --hours 168 --sort <span class="pl-k">time</span>
mychatarchive search <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>api design<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span> --since 2026-01-01</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Default mode is semantic (vector search). Supports: <code>--mode keyword</code> for FTS, <code>--platform</code> for source filter, <code>--hours</code> / <code>--since</code> for time filter, <code>--sort time</code> for newest-first, <code>--group</code> for group filter.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Export</h2><a id="user-content-export" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Export" href="#export"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="mychatarchive export archive.json           # full structured export
mychatarchive export archive.csv            # spreadsheet-friendly
mychatarchive export archive.db             # full SQLite copy with embeddings
mychatarchive export chatgpt.json --platform chatgpt
mychatarchive export everything.json --include-thoughts"><pre>mychatarchive <span class="pl-k">export</span> archive.json           <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> full structured export</span>
mychatarchive <span class="pl-k">export</span> archive.csv            <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> spreadsheet-friendly</span>
mychatarchive <span class="pl-k">export</span> archive.db             <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> full SQLite copy with embeddings</span>
mychatarchive <span class="pl-k">export</span> chatgpt.json --platform chatgpt
mychatarchive <span class="pl-k">export</span> everything.json --include-thoughts</pre></div>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Connecting to AI Tools</h2><a id="user-content-connecting-to-ai-tools" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Connecting to AI Tools" href="#connecting-to-ai-tools"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Claude Desktop</h3><a id="user-content-claude-desktop" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Claude Desktop" href="#claude-desktop"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="mychatarchive mcp-config --client claude-desktop"><pre>mychatarchive mcp-config --client claude-desktop</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Add the output to your config file:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>macOS:</strong> <code>~/Library/Application Support/Claude/claude_desktop_config.json</code></li>
<li><strong>Windows:</strong> <code>%APPDATA%\Claude\claude_desktop_config.json</code></li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Cursor</h3><a id="user-content-cursor" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Cursor" href="#cursor"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="mychatarchive mcp-config --client cursor"><pre>mychatarchive mcp-config --client cursor</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Add the output to your Cursor MCP settings.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Remote access via SSE</h3><a id="user-content-remote-access-via-sse" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Remote access via SSE" href="#remote-access-via-sse"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">For mobile or multi-device access, run the server on a NAS or always-on machine:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="mychatarchive serve --transport sse --port 8420"><pre>mychatarchive serve --transport sse --port 8420</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Connect via Tailscale or WireGuard from any device. Works with Claude mobile and any MCP client that supports remote servers.</p>
<hr>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">How It Works</h2><a id="user-content-how-it-works" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: How It Works" href="#how-it-works"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Stack</h3><a id="user-content-stack" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Stack" href="#stack"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Component</th>
<th>Technology</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><strong>Storage</strong></td>
<td>SQLite + FTS5 (full-text) + sqlite-vec (vectors)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>Embeddings</strong></td>
<td>sentence-transformers <code>all-MiniLM-L6-v2</code> (384 dim, local)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>Summarization</strong></td>
<td>Any OpenAI-compatible API (OpenRouter default, Anthropic fallback)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>Interface</strong></td>
<td>MCP server (stdio + SSE transport)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>Deduplication</strong></td>
<td>SHA1-based stable message IDs</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><strong>CLI</strong></td>
<td>Python argparse + rich</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Data stays local</h3><a id="user-content-data-stays-local" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Data stays local" href="#data-stays-local"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Embeddings run locally. No OpenAI, no cloud.</li>
<li>Database is a single SQLite file at <code>~/.mychatarchive/archive.db</code>.</li>
<li>MCP server runs over stdio by default (local pipe, no network).</li>
<li><code>summarize</code> is the only step that makes outbound API calls. It's optional.</li>
</ul>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">CLI Reference</h2><a id="user-content-cli-reference" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: CLI Reference" href="#cli-reference"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Command</th>
<th>Description</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>mychatarchive init</code></td>
<td>Interactive setup (drop folder, auto-discovery, backends)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>mychatarchive sync</code></td>
<td>Import from all sources (auto + drop folder + named)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>mychatarchive sync --embed</code></td>
<td>Sync + generate embeddings in one shot</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>mychatarchive import &lt;file|dir&gt;</code></td>
<td>Import a single file or directory</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>mychatarchive import --from &lt;name&gt;</code></td>
<td>Import from a named source</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>mychatarchive sources add &lt;name&gt; &lt;path&gt;</code></td>
<td>Add a named import source</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>mychatarchive sources list</code></td>
<td>Show all sources (auto + drop + named)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>mychatarchive sources remove &lt;name&gt;</code></td>
<td>Remove a source</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>mychatarchive sources rename &lt;old&gt; &lt;new&gt;</code></td>
<td>Rename a source</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>mychatarchive summarize</code></td>
<td>Generate LLM thread summaries (needs API key)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>mychatarchive groups list</code></td>
<td>List all thread groups</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>mychatarchive groups create &lt;name&gt;</code></td>
<td>Create a thread group</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>mychatarchive groups add &lt;group&gt; &lt;ids...&gt;</code></td>
<td>Add threads to a group</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>mychatarchive groups show &lt;name&gt;</code></td>
<td>Show threads in a group</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>mychatarchive groups delete &lt;name&gt;</code></td>
<td>Delete a group (threads are not deleted)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>mychatarchive classify --list</code></td>
<td>Show sensitivity counts per level</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>mychatarchive classify --thread &lt;id&gt; --level &lt;l&gt;</code></td>
<td>Classify a thread (public/private/sealed)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>mychatarchive classify --query &lt;text&gt; --level &lt;l&gt; --confirm</code></td>
<td>Bulk-classify threads by keyword</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>mychatarchive classify --before &lt;date&gt; --level &lt;l&gt; --confirm</code></td>
<td>Bulk-classify threads by age</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>mychatarchive embed</code></td>
<td>Generate vector embeddings</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>mychatarchive export &lt;output&gt;</code></td>
<td>Export to JSON, CSV, or SQLite copy</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>mychatarchive serve</code></td>
<td>Start MCP server</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>mychatarchive search &lt;query&gt;</code></td>
<td>Search from the terminal</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>mychatarchive info</code></td>
<td>Show archive statistics</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>mychatarchive mcp-config</code></td>
<td>Print MCP client configuration</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<p dir="auto">All commands accept <code>--db /path/to/archive.db</code> to override the default database location.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Project Structure</h2><a id="user-content-project-structure" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Project Structure" href="#project-structure"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

+-- tests/
