<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Adding a New Parser</h2><a id="user-content-adding-a-new-parser" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Adding a New Parser" href="#adding-a-new-parser"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Create <code>src/mychatarchive/parsers/yourplatform.py</code>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from typing import Iterator

def parse(input_path: str) -&gt; Iterator[dict]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;Yield normalized messages.&quot;&quot;&quot;
    yield {
        &quot;thread_id&quot;: &quot;unique-thread-id&quot;,
        &quot;thread_title&quot;: &quot;Conversation Title&quot;,
        &quot;role&quot;: &quot;user&quot;,
        &quot;content&quot;: &quot;Message text&quot;,
        &quot;created_at&quot;: 1700000000.0,
    }"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">typing</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">Iterator</span>

<span class="pl-k">def</span> <span class="pl-en">parse</span>(<span class="pl-s1">input_path</span>: <span class="pl-smi">str</span>) <span class="pl-c1">-&gt;</span> <span class="pl-v">Iterator</span>[<span class="pl-smi">dict</span>]:
    <span class="pl-s">"""Yield normalized messages."""</span>
    <span class="pl-k">yield</span> {
        <span class="pl-s">"thread_id"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"unique-thread-id"</span>,
        <span class="pl-s">"thread_title"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"Conversation Title"</span>,
        <span class="pl-s">"role"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"user"</span>,
        <span class="pl-s">"content"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"Message text"</span>,
        <span class="pl-s">"created_at"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">1700000000.0</span>,
    }</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Register it in <code>src/mychatarchive/parsers/__init__.py</code>.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Default Data Location</h2><a id="user-content-default-data-location" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Default Data Location" href="#default-data-location"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="~/.mychatarchive/
+-- archive.db          # SQLite database (messages + vectors + thoughts)
+-- config.json         # Backend + source configuration
+-- imports/            # Drop folder for export files"><pre class="notranslate"><code>~/.mychatarchive/
+-- archive.db          # SQLite database (messages + vectors + thoughts)
+-- config.json         # Backend + source configuration
+-- imports/            # Drop folder for export files
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Override with <code>--db /path/to/your.db</code> on any command, or set a custom drop folder path in <code>init</code>.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Roadmap</h2><a id="user-content-roadmap" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Roadmap" href="#roadmap"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul class="contains-task-list">
<li class="task-list-item"><input type="checkbox" id="" disabled="" class="task-list-item-checkbox" aria-label="Completed task" checked=""> Multi-platform import (ChatGPT, Claude, Grok, Claude Code, Cursor)</li>
<li class="task-list-item"><input type="checkbox" id="" disabled="" class="task-list-item-checkbox" aria-label="Completed task" checked=""> Local vector embeddings (sentence-transformers, no API)</li>
<li class="task-list-item"><input type="checkbox" id="" disabled="" class="task-list-item-checkbox" aria-label="Completed task" checked=""> MCP server: search_brain, search_recent, get_context, capture_thought, get_profile, get_current_datetime</li>
<li class="task-list-item"><input type="checkbox" id="" disabled="" class="task-list-item-checkbox" aria-label="Completed task" checked=""> Thread summaries via any OpenAI-compatible API (<code>mychatarchive summarize</code>)</li>
<li class="task-list-item"><input type="checkbox" id="" disabled="" class="task-list-item-checkbox" aria-label="Completed task" checked=""> Thread groups with group-scoped search (<code>mychatarchive groups</code>)</li>
<li class="task-list-item"><input type="checkbox" id="" disabled="" class="task-list-item-checkbox" aria-label="Completed task" checked=""> Platform, time, and group filters on search and all MCP tools</li>
<li class="task-list-item"><input type="checkbox" id="" disabled="" class="task-list-item-checkbox" aria-label="Completed task" checked=""> Pluggable backend architecture (storage, embeddings, transport)</li>
<li class="task-list-item"><input type="checkbox" id="" disabled="" class="task-list-item-checkbox" aria-label="Completed task" checked=""> Export (JSON, CSV, SQLite copy)</li>
<li class="task-list-item"><input type="checkbox" id="" disabled="" class="task-list-item-checkbox" aria-label="Completed task" checked=""> SSE transport for remote MCP access</li>
<li class="task-list-item"><input type="checkbox" id="" disabled="" class="task-list-item-checkbox" aria-label="Completed task" checked=""> One-command sync with auto-discovery + drop folder + named sources</li>
<li class="task-list-item"><input type="checkbox" id="" disabled="" class="task-list-item-checkbox" aria-label="Completed task" checked=""> Sensitivity levels: public / private / sealed with fail-closed retrieval (<code>mychatarchive classify</code>)</li>
<li class="task-list-item"><input type="checkbox" id="" disabled="" class="task-list-item-checkbox" aria-label="Incomplete task"> Additional parsers (Gemini, Perplexity, Copilot)</li>
<li class="task-list-item"><input type="checkbox" id="" disabled="" class="task-list-item-checkbox" aria-label="Incomplete task"> Grouping UI (browse threads and assign to groups without knowing thread IDs)</li>
<li class="task-list-item"><input type="checkbox" id="" disabled="" class="task-list-item-checkbox" aria-label="Incomplete task"> Analysis engine (deep prompts against your full archive)</li>
<li class="task-list-item"><input type="checkbox" id="" disabled="" class="task-list-item-checkbox" aria-label="Incomplete task"> Auto-sync (no manual exports needed)</li>
<li class="task-list-item"><input type="checkbox" id="" disabled="" class="task-list-item-checkbox" aria-label="Incomplete task"> PyPI publish</li>
<li class="task-list-item"><input type="checkbox" id="" disabled="" class="task-list-item-checkbox" aria-label="Incomplete task"> Web dashboard + hosted option at <a href="https://mychatarchive.com" rel="nofollow">mychatarchive.com</a></li>
<li class="task-list-item"><input type="checkbox" id="" disabled="" class="task-list-item-checkbox" aria-label="Incomplete task"> Docker image for one-command self-hosting</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">See <a href="/1ch1n/mychatarchive/blob/main/ROADMAP.md">ROADMAP.md</a> for the full phased plan.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Open Core</h2><a id="user-content-open-core" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Open Core" href="#open-core"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<thead>
<tr>
<th></th>
<th>Tier</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td>Import, embed, summarize, groups, MCP server (stdio)</td>
<td>Free / local (AGPL-3.0)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>SSE transport with auth, cloud sync, hosted MCP, teams</td>

</tr>

</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<p dir="auto">The principle: anything that runs on your machine is free. Anything that requires infrastructure is paid.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Licensing:</strong> Local and self-hosted use is free under AGPL-3.0. Commercial use or offering MyChatArchive as a hosted service requires a commercial license. Contact <a href="mailto:channing@mychatarchive.com">channing@mychatarchive.com</a> for commercial licensing.</p>
